TL;DR: 提出DESCENT，一种基于Transformer的架构，用于机场地面运动的轨迹预测，通过潜力可达集(PRS)上下文采样机制和检测Transformer解码器，在Amelia-10基准上超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商业航空交通密度增加，机场地面运行安全至关重要。虽然运动预测在自动驾驶领域已被充分研究，但机场地面运动预测仍缺乏探索。需要针对该领域异构动力学和严格拓扑约束的高效准确预测方法。

Method: 提出DESCENT架构，包含两个关键组件：(1) 潜力可达集(PRS)上下文采样机制，能够自适应地跨不同运行阶段收集机场环境上下文；(2) 基于检测Transformer的解码器，用于生成准确的轨迹预测。

Result: 在Amelia-10基准上的广泛评估显示，DESCENT相较于最先进的基线方法有显著性能提升，特别是在安全关键场景下，领域感知采样提供了安全导航所需的关键长时程上下文。

Conclusion: DESCENT通过领域感知的上下文采样和Transformer架构，有效解决了机场地面运动预测中异构动态和拓扑约束的挑战，在安全关键场景中表现出卓越性能，展示了该方法在提升机场地面运行安全性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Advanced automation is a key technology for enhancing the safety of ground operations amidst the increasing density of commercial air traffic. While motion forecasting is a well-studied task in autonomous driving, its application to airport surface movements remains underexplored. To enable efficient and accurate prediction in this domain, we propose DESCENT, a transformer-based architecture designed to handle heterogeneous dynamics and strict topological constraints. Our approach features a Potential Reachable Set (PRS) context sampling mechanism that adaptively collects airfield environment context across diverse operational phases. Combined with a detection transformer-based decoder, DESCENT generates accurate trajectory forecasts. Extensive evaluations on the Amelia-10 benchmark demonstrate significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. These gains are especially pronounced in safety-critical scenarios, where our domain-aware sampling provides critical long-horizon context necessary for safe navigation.

</details>


### [237] [Phantom Navigator: Stealthy and Precise Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Redirection with Real-Time Tracking and GPS Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26011)
*Haocheng Meng,Shaocheng Luo,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Phantom Navigator，一种结合离线可达性分析与在线闭环隐蔽执行层的无人机重定向攻击方法，通过LiDAR-相机检测与追踪堆栈实时识别、姿态估计并计算欺骗信号，实现隐蔽且精准地将目标无人机诱骗至指定位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机重定向攻击（物理捕获、通信劫持、传感器欺骗）存在可靠性低、精度差、隐蔽性不足等问题：物理拦截成本高且仅有一次机会，网络攻击需要深度专业知识，传感器欺骗难以达到精确性和鲁棒性。因此需要一种兼具隐蔽性、精确性和可靠性的无人机重定向攻击方法。

Method: Phantom Navigator采用两阶段方法：(1) 离线预重定向可达性分析，提供高保真的可达到重定向范围估计；(2) 在线闭环隐蔽执行层，确保实际场景中的成功重定向。同时构建了配备LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪和欺骗堆栈的物理攻击平台，实现实时识别、姿态估计和目标欺骗信号计算。

Result: 在真实世界案例研究中，验证了重定向方法的有效性和攻击实现的可行性，成功实现了对受害无人机隐蔽且精确的重定向。

Conclusion: Phantom Navigator填补了现有无人机重定向攻击在隐蔽性、精确性和可靠性方面的空白，通过离线分析与在线执行的结合，为无人机安全领域提供了新的攻击面视角和防御参考。

Abstract: Redirecting unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) from their intended mission trajectories has been an active area of research. However, existing UAV redirection attacks lack reliability, precision, and covertness for a targeted diversion. They primarily rely on physical capture, communication hijacking, or sensor spoofing. Yet, physical interception is costly, offers only a single opportunity for success, and poses a high risk of collateral damage; network-based attacks demand deep technical expertise and access to encrypted communication channels; and sensor spoofing techniques typically fall short in achieving the accuracy and robustness required to steer a UAV toward a specified target. Consequently, we propose Phantom Navigator, a UAV redirection attack to mislead drones to a designated spoofing target, covertly and precisely. Our approach combines offline pre-redirection reachability analysis, which provides high-fidelity estimates of achievable redirect ranges, with an online closed-loop, stealthy execution layer that ensures successful redirection in practice. Based on this approach, we build a physical attack platform equipped with a LiDAR--camera detection, tracking, and spoofing stack that performs real-time identification, pose estimation, and computation of targeted spoofing signals to covertly and accurately redirect victim UAVs to a designated location. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our redirection methodology and the attack implementation in real-world case studies.

</details>


### [238] [When Obstacles Bend: Modeling Vegetation Deformation in the context of Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26050)
*Muhammad Hsaeeb Zaar Khizar,Tom Montagnon,Roland Lenain,Romuald Aufrère,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出从植被本身的内在机械属性出发对其进行表征，而非从特定机器人平台的角度定义可通行性。通过结合形变测量与接触力数据，估算植被的底层机械参数并重建其交互响应，从而实现基于环境内在属性的植被感知导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的可通行性评估从机器人视角出发，将环境评估与机器人自身动力学耦合在一起，导致表征难以跨平台迁移，且不能直接反映植被本身的属性——而植被属性才是农业和环境监测等应用中交互与潜在损伤的真正来源。

Method: 将形变测量与接触力数据相结合，估算植被的底层机械参数，从而重建植被对交互作用的响应，实现对植被内在机械属性的独立表征。

Result: 提出了基于环境内在属性的植被感知导航方法，摆脱了对具体机器人平台的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过将植被表征从平台依赖的可通行性指标转变为基于内在机械属性的描述，可以实现更加通用、可迁移且能直接反映环境真实状态的导航决策。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in natural environments must often interact with vegetation rather than simply avoid it. In this context, traversability is typically defined from the robot's perspective, by measuring how a specific platform responds when moving through the environment. While practical, this viewpoint entangles the assessment of the environment with the robot's own dynamics, making the resulting characterization difficult to transfer across different platforms. More importantly, it does not directly reflect the properties of the vegetation itself, which are the true source of interaction and potential damage in applications such as agriculture and environmental monitoring. To address this limitation, we propose to characterize vegetation through its intrinsic mechanical properties, independently of any specific robot. By combining deformation measurements with contact force data, we estimate the underlying mechanical parameters and reconstruct the vegetation's response to interaction. This enables vegetation-aware navigation based on intrinsic environmental properties rather than platform-dependent metrics.

</details>


### [239] [$R^3$: Training Robots to Reason in Natural Language via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26053)
*Lehong Wu,Yuxiao Qu,Zheyuan Hu,Ivan Zhang,Limin Wei,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出R³，一种将现成视觉语言模型（VLM）训练为机器人推理器的后训练方法。该方法先在专家生成的推理轨迹上进行中期训练以初始化推理风格，再利用离线动作数据通过基于规则的强化学习（RL）进行改进。R³在Language Table和模拟双臂杂货打包两个基准上显著优于仅指令的模仿学习基线，表明自由形式的语言推理可作为测试时计算机制来引导底层操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言中的推理使基础模型能在困难问题上投入更多测试时计算，但该机制是否能改善机器人操作尚不明确。长时间跨度操作任务需要跟踪部分进度、推理物体关系、从错误中恢复并引导噪声底层策略，因此需要研究VLM能否通过自然语言推理直接引导底层操作策略。

Method: R³包含两个阶段：1）中期训练（mid-training）：在专家生成的推理轨迹上训练VLM，初始化期望的推理风格；2）基于规则的强化学习：从离线动作数据中，利用单步Rubric奖励对推理器进行改进。与先前使用结构化轨迹作为辅助监督的方法不同，R³训练自由形式的语言推理以生成测试时指导信号。

Result: 在Language Table和模拟双臂杂货打包两个测试平台上，R³改善了跨未见任务的探索和泛化能力，在两个基准上均显著优于仅指令的模仿学习基线。分析表明自由形式的语言推理可以作为测试时计算机制来引导底层策略。

Conclusion: R³验证了自由形式语言推理可作为机器人操作中有效的测试时计算机制，为将VLM转化为机器人推理器提供了简单有效的后训练方案。

Abstract: Reasoning in language allows foundation models to spend more test-time compute on hard problems, such as those requiring decomposition, constraint tracking, and prediction of future consequences. Whether this mechanism can improve robotic manipulation remains unclear, where long-horizon tasks require tracking partial progress, reasoning about object relations, recovering from mistakes, and steering noisy low-level policies. In this paper, we study whether VLMs can be trained to reason directly in natural language to guide low-level manipulation policies. We introduce $R^3$, a simple post-training recipe that turns off-the-shelf VLMs into robotic reasoners: it first mid-trains a VLM on expert-generated reasoning traces to initialize the desired reasoning style, then improves the reasoner with single-step rubric-based RL from offline action data. Unlike prior robotic reasoning methods that mostly use structured traces as auxiliary supervision, $R^3$ trains free-form language reasoning to produce test-time guidance for action. We instantiate $R^3$ on Language Table and simulated bimanual grocery packing, two controlled testbeds for studying robotic reasoning and long-horizon manipulation. $R^3$ improves exploration and generalization across unseen tasks and significantly outperforms instruction-only imitation learning baselines on both benchmarks. Our analyses suggest that free-form language reasoning can function as a test-time compute mechanism for steering low-level policies. Our project page is available at https://robotic-reasoner.github.io/.

</details>


### [240] [One Policy, Many Embodiments: Unified Camera-Centric Action Geometry Pre-training for Heterogeneous Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26058)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,University of Macau,:,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Guozhi Zhan,Zhixiang Duan,Yuhan Wang,Yuechen Luo,Shengyin Jiang,Hanbing Li,Zhiying Du,Longlong Wang,Longmei Jiang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yong Pan,Ziping Zhao,Zhiyuan Chen,Yangwei You,Kun Ma,Qinyuan Liu,Hangjun Ye,Zhi-xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UCAG-P提出了一种以相机为中心的统一定位动作公式（camera-centric unified action formulation），通过将异构具身数据集在几何层面进行对齐，使得单一VLA策略可以跨多种机器人形态、相机配置和动作空间进行联合学习，无需基准特定的微调即可在多个基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略的规模扩展严重受限于具身数据的异质性——不同机器人形态、相机配置和底层动作空间各不相同。已有的解决方法（显式动作重定向、人-机器人视频合成、数据集特定适配分支）都阻碍了统一策略的联合学习。

Method: UCAG-P的核心思想是将机器人特定的命令替换为相机可观测的锚点运动作为共享策略目标，在图像坐标和相机坐标系中表示操作。其采用几何条件动作翻译器（geometry-conditioned action translator），将预测的运动与目标具身的运动学结合生成可执行控制。这种解耦架构使得共享VLA策略能够学习可迁移的操作几何，同时保留具身特定的可控性。训练数据包括4.03K小时机器人和仿真数据，以及2.34K小时人类演示数据。

Result: 单个检查点在无基准特定微调的情况下达到：LIBERO 98.3%，RoboTwin Easy和Hard分别为88.7%和89.2%，LIBERO-Plus零样本82.0%，RoboCasa GR-1上62.0%。

Conclusion: UCAG-P通过相机中心化的动作表示有效解决了具身数据异构问题，实现了跨多具身形态的统一VLA策略学习，展示了将其身操作行为解耦为"共享几何+具身特定运动学"的可行性。

Abstract: Scaling generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies is severely bottlenecked by the inherent heterogeneity of embodied data, which spans diverse robot morphologies, camera configurations, and low-level action spaces. Existing paradigms typically address this mismatch through explicit action retargeting, human-to-robot video synthesis, or dataset-specific adaptation branches, fundamentally hindering the joint learning of a unified policy. We introduce UCAG-P, a camera-centric unified action formulation that structurally aligns heterogeneous embodied datasets into a shared geometric action space. Rather than treating robot-specific commands as the shared policy target, UCAG-P represents manipulation through camera-observable anchor motion in image and camera-frame coordinates, treating robot arms, humanoids, and human hands as different embodiments of a common action schema. A geometry-conditioned action translator combines predicted motion with target-embodiment kinematics to produce executable controls. The resulting decoupled architecture allows a shared VLA policy to learn transferable manipulation geometry while retaining embodiment-specific controllability. UCAG-P is trained on 4.03K hours of robot and simulation data and 2.34K hours of human demonstrations. A single checkpoint reaches 98.3% on LIBERO, 88.7% and 89.2% on RoboTwin Easy and Hard, 82.0% zero-shot on LIBERO-Plus, and 62.0% on RoboCasa GR-1, without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [241] [VirTooS: A ROS 2 - Unity Virtualization Toolkit for Fleet Management of Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26066)
*Andrea Drudi,Lorenzo Pichierri,Andrea Testa,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VirTooS 是一个基于 Python/C# 的工具包，利用 ROS 2 和 Unity 游戏引擎，为自主移动机器人（AMR）团队提供混合现实环境下的仿真与车队管理任务支持。该工具包支持虚拟/真实传感器（如 LiDAR）导航、通过 ChoiRbot 框架实现分布式实验，并附带容器化部署方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人车队管理任务需要在可扩展、逼真的虚拟环境中进行验证，但现有的仿真工具在混合现实场景下缺乏灵活性、可定制性和分布式支持，因此需要一个集成的工具包来弥合虚拟仿真与真实机器人实验之间的鸿沟。

Method: VirTooS 采用 ROS 2 与 Unity 游戏引擎相结合的方式构建混合现实环境；提供自定义虚拟场景生成能力；支持虚拟与真实 LiDAR 等传感器的集成用于建图和导航；利用 ChoiRbot 框架设计分布式机器人实验例程；并附带容器化套件以支持跨机器便捷部署。

Result: 通过任务分配问题（Task Assignment）的系列实验展示了虚拟环境中真实机器人与虚拟机器人之间的无缝交互能力，验证了工具包在混合现实环境中的可行性和实用性。源代码将公开在 GitHub 上。

Conclusion: VirTooS 为 AMR 车队管理提供了一套开源的、可扩展且易部署的混合现实仿真工具，能够有效支持分布式机器人实验，未来有望成为机器人仿真与真实世界融合研究的基础平台。

Abstract: In this paper, we present VirTooS, a Python/C# toolkit designed to implement fleet-management tasks on teams of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). VirTooS leverages the Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 and Unity game engine to provide realistic, scalable virtual experiments in a mixed-reality environment. The toolbox allows users to easily generate and customize virtual scenarios for realistic simulations. Virtual and real sensors as, e.g., LiDARs, can be exploited to map and safely navigate in the mixed-reality environment. To enable distributed robotics experiments, we propose a set of tailored routines leveraging the ChoiRbot framework. As a motivating example, we show a set of experiments for task assignment problems in a virtual environment, allowing seamless interaction among real and virtual robots. Moreover, the package comes with a containerized suite to easily deploy it on different machines. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [242] [Gating Before Commitment: Anticipating Intent Divergence to Prevent Post-Interaction Decision Failures in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26074)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究车辆交互中的意图误解导致规划失败的问题，提出一个由语言引导的意图模块驱动的决策门控层：该模块读取结构化描述、计算平滑的意图-几何散度分数，并在通道包络（corridor envelope）上游对规划机动进行门控。在离线越野偏离场景和四个碰撞片段的重放中（冻结、已披露的实现），门控是唯一能够修复规划失败的层级：主案例在漂移开始后72ms触发，但在通道退出前161ms，所有十次重放均保持在通道内。首次校准在5.9分钟内产生9次误触发（源于将评分不确定性计为半次冲突）；预先注册的重设计将不确定性视为弃权，将误触发降至每分钟0.341次。消融实验界定模型贡献：全评分在部署资格下对五个失败检测最快（四个），对未否决规则下三个；几何规则在等误报下检测率提升三倍以上。证据支持门控机制有效性，模型的作用是快速检测与对几何规则的不确定性否决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆交互中意图被误解会导致规划反复失败，需要一种能够在机动承诺之前识别并阻止错误规划的决策机制。作者希望在传统几何包络检测之外，引入一个由语言引导的意图推理模块，通过门控层在规划提交前拦截潜在的危险机动，从而提高自动驾驶规划的鲁棒性和安全性。

Method: 本研究构建了一个三层决策架构：最底层为走廊包络（corridor envelope）约束；中层为语言引导的意图模块，该模块读取结构化的驾驶意图描述符，计算一个平滑的意图-几何散度评分（intent-geometry divergence score）；顶层为门控层，在机动提交（commitment）之前根据评分裁决是否放行或否决该机动。作者在冻结节点（frozen node）和已披露实现（disclosed implementation）下进行离线重放实验，评估门控层的检测速度、误触发率，并通过消融实验（去除信心项、对比纯几何规则）量化各组件贡献。首次校准中评分不确定性被计为半次冲突导致高误触发，随后通过预先注册的重新设计将不确定性处理为弃票（abstention）来降低误触发。

Result: 门控层是唯一能修复规划失败的层级：主案例平均在漂移开始后72ms触发、通道退出前161ms触发，十次重放全部保持轨迹在通道内。首次校准在5.9分钟内产生9次误触发（不确定性被计为半次冲突所致），重新设计后降至0.341次/分钟。消融结果表明：全评分在部署资格下对五个失败中的四个检测最快，未否决规则下三个；去掉置信项损失两次检测；对比基线显示门控的贡献主要在于快速检测及对几何规则的不确定性否决；在等误报条件下几何规则单独使用时检测率提升超过三倍，说明几何规则本身有效但需要意图模块以不确定性形式进行否决来消除误报。

Conclusion: 证据支持门控机制的有效性：它能够在规划提交前及时拦截危险机动，且检测速度快于纯几何规则。模型的具体贡献被界定为两方面：（1）对这些失败场景提供最快的检测；（2）通过对几何规则的打分不确定性实施否决（veto），从而减少误触发。同时指出某些触发无法被现有五段片段明确分类为信号还是巧合，提示未来需要在更大、更多样的数据集上验证。

Abstract: Intent misinterpretation during vehicle interactions causes recurring planning failures. We study a decision layer in which a language-guided intent module reads structured descriptors, computes a smoothed intent-geometry divergence score, and gates the planned maneuver before commitment, upstream of a corridor envelope. On a replayed off-road departure and four crash clips under a frozen, disclosed implementation, gating is the only layer that repairs the plan: on the main case it fires 72 ms after the drift onset but 161 ms before the corridor exit, keeping the trajectory in the corridor in all ten replays. The first calibration draws nine false triggers in 5.9 minutes, each from scoring uncertainty as half a conflict; a preregistered redesign treating uncertainty as abstention cuts this to 0.341 per minute. Two ablations bound the model's contribution: the full score detects fastest on four of five failures under the deployed eligibility, three of five against the unvetoed rule (000871 by one cycle; 000228 by a pre-onset fire on an uncertain stretch that five clips cannot classify as signal or coincidence; dropping the confidence term costs two detections), while on in-domain tracks at equal false positives the geometric rule more than triples its detection. The evidence supports the gating mechanism; the model's demonstrated roles are the fastest detection on these failures and an uncertainty veto on the geometric rule.

</details>


### [243] [Fast Generative Grasping via Lie Group-Constrained MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26076)
*S. Talha Bukhari,Yi Wei,Ruiqi Ni,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于李群约束MeanFlow的快速生成式抓取方法，在乘积李群SO(3)×R³上进行轨迹对齐与代数半群一致性约束，仅需≤5次网络评估即可生成可靠抓取，较扩散/流模型提速39倍，并在真实机器人上无需微调即可直接部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式抓取（如扩散、流模型）虽能建模抓取的多模态分布并具备泛化性，但其迭代多步采样开销大，难以满足机器人抓取的时间关键性要求。

Method: 在乘积李群G=SO(3)×R³上设计基于MeanFlow的方法：训练目标结合纯代数半群一致性条件与G上的黎曼条件流匹配（Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching），将平均速度锚定到数据分布，形成李群约束的MeanFlow公式，实现极少数网络评估即可完成采样。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上，方法以毫秒级推理延迟（最高39倍加速）匹配甚至超越最先进的扩散及流模型抓取生成性能；并在真实机器人抓取中无需额外训练或域适应，对观测噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 李群约束的MeanFlow框架在保持生成式抓取质量的同时大幅压缩采样步数，兼顾了生成模型的灵活性与机器人操作所需的实时性，且具备良好的真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis is a core task in robotic manipulation, for which the solution typically forms a multimodal distribution rather than a point estimate. Generative robotic grasping aims to learn this distribution with deep generative models such as diffusion and flow-based approaches. The iterative nature of such generative models makes them flexible and generalizable; however, multi-step sampling impedes the time-critical operation required in robotics. We devise an approach to fast generative grasping based on MeanFlow on the product Lie group $\mathcal{G} = \mathrm{SO}(3) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. The training objective couples a purely algebraic semigroup consistency condition with Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching on $\mathcal{G}$ that anchors the average velocity to the data distribution. The resulting Lie Group-constrained MeanFlow formulation samples reliable grasps in $\leq 5$ network evaluations, matching the grasp generation performance of state-of-the-art diffusion and flow-based models on the ACRONYM dataset at millisecond-scale inference latency (up to $39\times$ speed-up). We further demonstrate that the approach directly translates to real-world robotic grasping without additional training or domain adaptation, exhibiting robust grasp synthesis under observation noise.

</details>


### [244] [Zero-WAM: In-Context World-Action Modeling from Human Videos for Open-Ended Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26103)
*Jiaming Zhou,Qihang Zhang,Gangwei Xu,Cunxin Fan,Yujie Zhao,Ruilin Wang,Yiming Luo,Shuai Yang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Junwei Liang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zero-WAM是一种零样本跨任务泛化的因果视频-动作模型，通过将人类视频作为上下文提示（in-context learning），让机器人策略执行训练中从未见过的操作任务。在RoboTwin 2.0仿真中，7个未见任务上平均成功率47.0%，比最强基线绝对提升29.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本跨任务泛化是机器人学习中的核心挑战。受大语言模型中通过上下文指定任务即可执行新任务的启发，作者提出将"上下文学习"范式引入机器人操作领域，并论证人类视频比语言更适合作为操作任务的自然规格说明，因为视频提供丰富的视觉线索来传达任务演化过程。

Method: 提出Zero-WAM，一个因果视频-动作模型，通过上下文人类视频引导执行未见任务。针对任务丰富的配对人类-机器人数据稀缺问题，提出自动pipeline将任务采样的机器人轨迹转换为语义匹配的人类视频，构建HumanGen数据集（74.2K人类-机器人ICL对，8.6K任务）。训练中引入上下文未来分块预测（IFP）目标，抑制从已见任务中学到的捷径，迫使策略从视频提示中提取任务信息。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0仿真中，Zero-WAM在7个未见任务上平均成功率达47.0%，较最强的视频-动作基线绝对提升29.5个百分点。真实世界评估中，Zero-WAM能跟随人类视频引导，泛化到多物体场景、长时程操作和精细插入等未见任务配置。

Conclusion: Zero-WAM成功将大语言模型中的上下文学习范式迁移到机器人操作领域，论证了人类视频作为操作任务规格说明的有效性，并通过专项数据集构建和IFP训练目标实现了显著的零样本跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot cross-task generalization, where a policy must execute manipulation tasks never seen during training, remains a central challenge in robot learning. In large language models, a novel task can be performed simply by specifying it in the context, without any parameter update. This form of in-context learning (ICL) turns generalization into a problem of task specification. To achieve cross-task generalization, we bring this paradigm to robotic manipulation, and argue that the natural task specification for manipulation is a human video: unlike language, it provides rich visual cues about the intended task evolution. We present Zero-WAM, a causal video-action model that executes unseen tasks by following in-context human video guidance. To address the scarcity of task-rich paired human-robot data, we propose an automatic pipeline that converts task-sampled robot trajectories into semantically matched human videos, yielding HumanGen, a dataset of 74.2K human-robot ICL pairs across 8.6K tasks. For model training, we further introduce an in-context future chunk prediction (IFP) objective that suppresses shortcuts learned from seen tasks and forces the policy to draw task information from the video prompt. On seven unseen tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 simulation, Zero-WAM achieves a 47.0% average success rate, an absolute improvement of 29.5 percentage points over the strongest video-action baseline. In real-world evaluations, it follows human video guidance to generalize to unseen task configurations involving multi-object scenes, long-horizon manipulation, and fine-grained insertion.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，利用四个同步IMU（训练时可用的多位置传感器信息）来提升仅使用右臂单个IMU的学生模型的识别性能，通过为logit和特征蒸馏损失分别分配样本级门控权重，在WEAR数据集上将macro-F1从0.5716提升至0.6385。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多位置惯性传感器可以提升活动识别精度，但推理阶段要求所有传感器同时工作会增加部署负担。作者希望利用训练时多IMU的信息来增强仅用单IMU推理的学生模型，同时发现固定权重的知识蒸馏对每个样本施加相同强度的目标，忽略了学生可能从不同教师目标中获益不均的问题。

Method: 采用冻结的四IMU教师模型为单IMU（右臂）学生提供logit和特征蒸馏目标。提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法：在独立的fold内部训练参与者上测试单步候选更新，评估损失权重对模型性能的影响，从而为每个训练样本的logit损失和特征损失分别分配样本级门控权重。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上（19个标签、68,298个完整窗口、22名参与者，五折交叉验证），Supervised基线macro-F1为0.5618，固定权重KD为0.5716，DIW达到0.6385，分别提升7.66和6.68个百分点。DIW在19个标签中的18个、22位参与者中的21位上超过Supervised。所有方法推理时保持同一80,915参数的右臂学生模型。

Conclusion: DIW能够在不改变部署时的传感配置和学生前向计算图的情况下，将训练阶段的多位置传感器信息有效转化为更强的单IMU模型，显著提升活动识别性能，验证了训练-推理不对称场景下动态知识蒸馏权重的有效性。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [246] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny，一个0.6B参数的法律领域嵌入模型，在MLEB基准上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B参数以下的模型中表现出竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域检索需要专门的嵌入模型，而大型模型在资源受限环境中难以部署。作者旨在通过领域特定的训练方法，在紧凑模型规模下实现高性能的法律文本检索能力。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：首先从大型教师模型蒸馏知识到紧凑的学生架构，然后进行结合硬负样本挖掘的领域特定微调；使用包含340万查询-段落对的数据集（含15万人工精选样本，覆盖多个法律司法管辖区）；支持多级量化（BF16、INT8、二进制）的高效推理架构。

Result: GreenLeaf-Tiny在MLEB上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B参数以下的嵌入模型中表现出色，且支持多种量化级别以适用于资源受限环境。

Conclusion: 领域特定的训练与高质量数据相结合，能够显著提升专业领域应用的嵌入模型性能，即使模型规模较小也能获得有竞争力的检索效果。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [247] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动的视角全面综述了多模态异常检测（MMAD），将现有工作组织为"正态性假设方法"和"异常性假设方法"两大互补范式，并探讨了基础模型对MMAD的变革性影响，最后总结了各领域基准数据集、评估协议及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMAD在工业检测和网络安全等安全关键应用中日益重要，但现有文献在不同领域和模态组合间高度碎片化，且已有综述多按架构而非异常的定义和分离方式对方法进行归类，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解该领域。

Method: 采用假设驱动视角，形式化MMAD问题定义，识别支撑其核心挑战的五种内在特征，将前期工作划分为两大互补范式：正态性假设方法（通过表示学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模规律性）和异常性假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构化和语义异常注入来锐化决策边界）。同时考察基础模型通过可扩展预训练、灵活跨模态迁移和推理能力对MMAD的重塑作用。

Result: 编译了跨领域的代表性基准数据集和评估协议，系统梳理了两大范式下的方法体系，阐明了基础模型在MMAD中的应用现状，并指出了开放问题和未来方向。

Conclusion: MMAD领域需要更鲁棒、自适应和可解释的系统，未来研究应聚焦于统一理论框架的完善、基础模型能力的深入利用以及跨领域标准化评估体系的建设。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [248] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExFold是一个统一的训练免微调的专家折叠框架，通过将预算外的专家贡献以标量投影器折叠进保留专家中，同时加速MoE预填充和解码阶段，在vLLM中实现了最高1.41x的TTFT和2.45x的TPOT加速，同时保持约99%的平均质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE加速方法只优化单一资源代理（要么优化每个token执行的专家，要么优化批次激活的专家集），且要么丢弃被排除专家的贡献，要么仅隐式近似。预填充和解码阶段存在根本不同的瓶颈（预填充受限于token级专家计算，解码受限于批次级激活专家集的内存流量），缺乏统一的高效加速方案。

Method: ExFold将预填充和解码统一视为一个带预算的输出近似问题：只执行阶段特定的受限专家集，同时通过校准的标量投影器将预算外专家的贡献折叠到保留专家上。核心发现是许多专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同，因此ExFold在无标签数据上校准成对标量投影器矩阵，推理时用于折叠排除专家的贡献。预填充加速变为token级Top-K折叠，解码加速变为批次级专家池折叠，两阶段共享同一折叠机制，仅在保留专家的选择方式上不同。实现了即插即用的vLLM插件和轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核。

Result: 在vLLM中作为即插即用插件实现，使用轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，实现了最高1.41x的TTFT（首token时间）和2.45x的TPOT（每输出token时间）加速，同时保留了约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold通过统一的专家折叠框架同时解决预填充和解码的加速问题，验证了"许多专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同"这一观察的有效性，为MoE推理加速提供了一个训练免微调、可插拔的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [249] [The Von-Neumann State-Space Transformer for neural decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25088)
*Morteza Sarafyazd*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出冯·诺依曼状态空间Transformer（VN-SST），一种受冯·诺依曼架构启发、以低秩指令库替代标准前馈层的Transformer变体。该模型通过每个token从低维状态空间记忆中读取指令代码合成权重矩阵，实现数据高效和参数高效的神经解码，在运动皮层神经解码基准上大幅优于现代Transformer，并在小规模语言建模任务上也展现更优的参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层计算具有低维特性——少量潜在变量即可调控高维神经元响应。研究目标是提高样本效率（小数据、小参数量下良好解码）。标准Transformer前馈块对每个token施加相同算子，而作者提出冯·诺依曼式的高效计算假说：控制器解码指令后执行token特定的算子，这比仅混合输出（如软混合专家）更为高效。

Method: VN-SST的核心是将Transformer前馈块改造为低秩指令库（instruction bank）：由一个共享基算子加一组低秩学习的指令组成，每个token通过从携带的状态空间记忆的低维投影中读取代码，合成该token实际使用的权重矩阵。慢速潜在轨迹充当指令指针，模拟低维动力学对皮层计算的路径路由。

Result: 在三个运动皮层神经解码基准上，VN-SST相比现代Transformer数据效率显著更高，在数据最稀缺的基准上大幅领先，另外两个基准上也领先；且能将更长上下文转化为精度上升而非下降。网络将大型指令库压缩为每token仅数比特，程序容量作为控制通道而非精度杠杆。在语言建模的小型文本基准上同样更参数高效。

Conclusion: VN-SST验证了冯·诺依曼式高效计算假说的可行性：通过低维控制信号路由token特定算子，能够在神经解码中以极高的数据与参数效率超越标准Transformer架构，并展现出跨领域（神经解码与语言建模）的通用机制，为类脑高效计算架构提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cortical computation is strikingly low-dimensional: a handful of latent variables, carried in a neural population's activity, steer the higher-dimensional responses of individual neurons. Our aim is sample efficiency-models that decode well from limited data and at small parameter budgets. In a standard Transformer layer, the feed-forward block applies the same operator to every token. We suggest a von-Neumann inspired hypothesis of efficient computation as an alternative for neural decoding: a controller decodes an instruction and then executes a token-specific operator; the usual realization-a soft mixture of experts-only blends their outputs, not operators. We introduce a von-Neumann State-Space Transformer (VN-SST), a memory-augmented Transformer whose feed-forward block is a low-rank instruction bank: a shared base operator plus a small set of learned low-rank instructions, from which a per-token code synthesizes the weight matrix actually used at that token. The code is read from a low- dimensional projection of a carried state-space memory, so a slow latent trajectory acts as an instruction pointer-mirroring how low-dimensional dynamics may route cortical computation. On three motor-cortex neural-decoding benchmarks, VN-SST is far more data-efficient than a modern Transformer, each jointly predicting spikes and decoding behavior. This model wins by a wide margin on the scarcest benchmark, leads on the other two, and turns longer context into rising rather than falling accuracy. We evaluated that the network compresses a large instruction bank to a few bits per token, so program capacity acts as a control channel, not an accuracy lever. The same model is also more parameter-efficient on two small text benchmarks used for language modeling (LLMs), suggesting a generic mechanism.

</details>


### [250] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双分支、频谱门控的物理信息神经网络（DBSG-PINN），将低频和高频分量拆分为独立子网络并通过自适应门控连接，用于缓解PINN的频谱偏差问题。在五个一维基准PDE上进行的部分受控消融实验表明：频率分解在频谱复杂的基准上效果显著（多模态波问题相对L2误差降低59.2%），但在光滑问题上收益甚微，且在一维波动方程上表现劣于更简单的固定组合变体。门控的收益与目标解的频谱丰富度正相关。由于仅使用单个训练种子，该研究定位为探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN虽将控制方程直接嵌入训练，但存在频谱偏差——网络学习低频分量快于高频分量。现有技术（如傅里叶特征嵌入、正弦激活）通常假设对所有频谱区间都有帮助，但缺少系统性验证。本文旨在通过频谱分解和频谱路由的受控消融，探究不同频率分解策略在不同频谱特征PDE上的实际收益边界。

Method: 提出DBSG-PINN架构：将输入分解为低频和高频两路，分别送入独立子网络，通过自适应门控（adaptive gate）将两路输出融合。对频率分解和频谱路由两个组件进行部分受控消融实验，在五个一维基准PDE（覆盖光滑单尺度到振荡多尺度问题）上进行评估。

Result: 频率分解在频谱复杂的基准上效果显著，多模态波问题相对L2误差降低59.2%；在更光滑的PDE上几乎无收益；在一维波动方程上性能显著差于固定组合变体。门控的收益与目标解频谱丰富度正相关，全模型相对消融版本的优势在多尺度基准上最大、单尺度基准上最小甚至为负。

Conclusion: 该探索性研究揭示了频谱分解和频谱路由并非对所有PDE一致有益——其价值高度依赖目标解的频谱结构。研究受限于单一训练种子和五个一维基准，属于提出问题而非得出结论的探索性工作，未来需要更多种子和基准来验证所观察到的模式是否稳定成立。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [251] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Fisher信息的自适应混合精度权重量化方法（FAMPWQ），通过层自适应位宽分配实现大语言模型在资源受限设备上的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在多个领域取得显著成就，但过高的资源需求阻碍了其在资源受限设备上的部署。传统量化方法由于采用均匀位宽或简单的启发式敏感性评估，通常会导致严重的性能退化。因此需要一种更精确的敏感性度量和自适应的混合精度量化方案。

Method: FAMPWQ包含两个核心组件：(1) 提出一个基于Fisher信息度量的系统模型，用于衡量逐层对量化的敏感性；(2) 提出一个基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感性度量生成自适应的位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准上进行了大量实验，与7个基线方法相比，FAMPWQ在PPL上降低多达3.39，准确率提高多达6.87%，LLM-as-a-judge比较中胜率高达76%。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息驱动的自适应混合精度量化，显著优于现有基线方法，为LLM在商品级GPU上的高效推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [252] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MacroAgent是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的四阶段宏单元合法性化（macro legalization）框架，通过聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间精修四个步骤自动发现鲁棒且高效的启发式算法，在布局规律性、布线线长和PPA方面显著优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏单元占据核心区域的大部分，且宏单元位置对最终QoR有显著影响。然而现有宏单元合法性化方法存在鲁棒性不足、计算成本高、忽视宏单元间规律性等问题。

Method: 提出MacroAgent四阶段框架：1）聚类（clustering）将宏单元分组；2）轮廓生成（contour generation）利用LLM发现多种有效的启发式规律性感知算法生成布局轮廓；3）模板匹配（template matching）选择合适模板；4）簇间精修（inter-cluster refinement）优化簇间布局，从而生成鲁棒高效的宏单元合法性化算法方案。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准上，相比最先进方法：布局规律性提升2-8倍，布线线长降低3%-5%，拥塞相当，鲁棒性显著提升且运行时间可接受。端到端Cadence Innovus评估显示：相对DREAMPlace基线布线线长降低2.9%、TNS改善68.3%；集成到Innovus宏单元布局流程中布线线长降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent证明了LLM能够有效发现宏单元合法性化中的启发式规律性感知算法，为EDA领域利用大语言模型进行自动化算法设计提供了可行性验证，并在布局规律性、线长和PPA等多个维度取得显著提升。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [253] [Interpreting Protein Language Model Embeddings via Orthogonal Projection for Protein Fitness Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25548)
*Paulo Yanez Sarmiento,Pia Francesca Rissom,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marco Simnacher,Jordan F. Safer,Sumaiya Iqbal,Henrike O. Heyne,Nadja Klein,Bernhard Y. Renard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于正交投影的技术，用于从蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的嵌入中移除已知生化特征的线性、高阶及交互效应，从而量化这些可解释特征对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献。消融实验显示移除这些特征后下游分类器性能下降，证明PLM嵌入确实编码了与生化特性相关的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLM）的嵌入在蛋白质适应性预测等下游任务中取得了最先进的表现，但这些嵌入本身不可直接解释，人们不清楚它们具体编码了哪些特征。为了理解哪些生化特性在驱动预测结果，需要一种方法从嵌入中剥离可解释的生化特征并量化其贡献。

Method: 作者利用正交投影技术，将已知表格特征（即生化性质）从PLM嵌入中移除，并将这一方法从线性效应扩展到高阶效应和交互效应。通过消融研究，比较移除这些特征前后下游分类器在蛋白质适应性预测任务上的性能差异，以评估生化特征的贡献。

Result: 实验结果表明，从PLM嵌入中移除生化特征后，下游分类器的蛋白质适应性预测性能显著下降；进一步的评估显示这些生化特征解释了该分类器预测结果中的很大一部分方差。从而证明了PLM嵌入编码了与生化性质相关的模式，并量化了它们对蛋白质适应性预测的贡献。

Conclusion: 该正交投影方法在计算上高效，不局限于特定的特征或嵌入类型，可以便捷地迁移到蛋白质适应性预测之外的其他问题场景，为可解释PLM嵌入提供了一种通用的量化分析工具。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a growing adoption of protein language models (PLMs) in biomedical science. Their embeddings provide a rich numerical representation of protein sequences which achieve state-of-the-art performance on several downstream tasks including protein fitness prediction. However, PLM embeddings are not directly interpretable and, thereby, it remains unclear what features they encode. To gain insight into which biochemical properties of the protein are driving the prediction, we leverage an orthogonal projection technique that removes linear effects of known tabular features from embeddings and extend it to high-order and interaction effects. In this way, we remove the effects of interpretable biochemical features from PLM embeddings. In an ablation study, we show that this leads to a decrease in performance for a downstream classifier trained only on the embeddings to predict protein fitness. In an additional evaluation, we find that these biochemical features explain a substantial part of the variance in the predictions of this classifier. Hence, we can show that PLM embeddings encode patterns correlated with biochemical properties and quantify their contribution to predicting protein fitness. This computationally efficient approach is not limited to the features or embeddings considered here and is readily transferable to problem settings beyond protein fitness prediction.

</details>


### [254] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAT-GS，一种作用于反向传播过程的神经动力学优化控制器，通过校准教师置信度、阈值化门控策略和融合层梯度手术（fusion-only PCGrad）来解决多模态神经网络训练中的模态失衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰三大耦合失效模式，无需修改模型架构、融合模块或任务损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态神经网络的端到端训练常表现出不稳定的神经动力学，表现为三种耦合的失败模式：模态失衡（某一分支主导梯度优化）、不稳定门控（噪声置信度导致模态选择紊乱）和融合干扰（模态特定梯度在共享融合层相互冲突），这些都会降低学习效果。

Method: CAT-GS在反向传播阶段工作，具体包括：(1) 通过温度缩放和EMA平滑校准教师派生的可靠性；(2) 采用基于margin阈值的策略在warm-up dropout、弱模态优先和弱偏置混合三种模式间切换以稳定动力学；(3) 通过封顶的梯度预算重归一化稳定激进门控下的梯度幅值；(4) 仅在融合层应用PCGrad减少破坏性跨模态干扰。

Result: 在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound、UR-FUNNY、CG-MNIST、AVE和CMU-MOSI等多模态基准上，CAT-GS相比于OGM-GE、G²D和UMT等强不平衡感知基线，在融合多模态准确率上实现改进或持平，并获得更平滑的门控行为和更少的冲突融合梯度。

Conclusion: CAT-GS作为一种无需修改架构的优化级干预手段，能有效缓解多模态训练中的不稳定动力学，在不改变模型设计的前提下提升融合性能并改善门控稳定性。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [255] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统拆解了RL（强化学习）后训练算法的各个环节，在受控简化环境中隔离各组件，阐明RL后训练成功背后的机制与先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练虽已成为提升LLM能力（推理、数学、编码）的强大框架，但许多研究者和从业者对其背后的经典RL原理仍视为"黑盒"。作者希望揭开这一黑盒，提供一份可操作的入门指南。

Method: 在受控且简化的环境中，使用Verifiable Rewards（可验证奖励）的RL机制，逐个隔离并考察四个关键因素对后训练结果的影响：基础模型的先验分布、奖励信号的粒度、提示分布的多样性、以及模型规模。以策略输出分布的熵作为透镜，比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练各阶段所学习的分布。

Result: 研究发现RL后训练的效果取决于所选因素的交互作用。具体包括：'虚假奖励'（spurious rewards）的影响取决于后训练所用的提示分布；RL后训练是否成功取决于基础模型是否已在目标行为上分配了足够的概率质量，这与经典RL中的探索概念相关联。

Conclusion: 本文为NLP社区提供了一份关于如何将RL作为工具纳入工具箱的入门资源（primer），帮助研究者理解RL后训练背后机制及其成功条件。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [256] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AFDBench，首个评估生成式气象推理能力的基准，包含7,732篇NWS专家撰写的区域预报讨论（AFD）及配套AI天气预测数据。通过GRPO强化学习优化7B参数模型，使模型在风格对齐度上从0.318提升至0.619，输入对齐度从0.881提升至0.940，使小规模开源模型能够以专业气象学家的行文风格忠实解读AI气象数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生成高风险的天气预报文本时会产生数值幻觉，给天气通信带来风险。现有模型无法以专业NWS机构（美国国家气象局）的正式语域撰写气象预报讨论，也难以忠实使用输入的结构化AI气象数据。需要一个专门的基准来评估生成式气象推理能力。

Method: 构建AFDBench基准，包含来自13个NWS办事处的7,732篇专家撰写的AFD文本与Google WeatherNext 2结构化预报数据配对。设计三个互补评估指标：Met-Align（数值准确率）、Style-Align（专业语域遵循度）和Input-Grounding（对源数据保真度）。采用零样本评估开源LLM，并应用GRPO（组相对策略优化）配合领域特定奖励函数，覆盖温度准确性、天气形势正确性和格式符合性。

Result: 零样本评估显示开源LLM的Style-Align较低（约0.33），Input-Grounding中等（约0.88）。在1,033个来自两个未见过的NWS办事处的测试样本上，GRPO使Style-Align从0.318近乎翻倍提升至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。

Conclusion: 强化学习（GRPO）能够有效训练7B参数的小型模型，使其以专业气象学家的语域写作并忠实解读AI气象数据，验证了强化学习在专业领域文本生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [257] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出神经代理模型在无导数优化中的矛盾结果源于三个被忽略的因素：角色（代理提出候选解而非替代梯度则有帮助）、半径（代理仅在局部邻域内可靠）、余地（代理只能加速基方法本可实现的进展）。通过117个基准实例的系统实验验证了这三个因素，并提出基于半径感知的局部泛化理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中关于神经代理模型在无导数优化中的效果存在矛盾：同一类模型对一个求解器减少评估次数，对另一个却无效甚至更差。本文旨在揭示这一矛盾背后的根本原因，而非简单地归因于拟合精度。

Method: 提出三个决定性因素并进行形式化：（1）角色——代理提出候选解由真实目标函数审批则有益，代理替代求解器依赖的梯度则有害；（2）半径——拟合于优化轨迹的模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，误差既不随邻域收缩而消失，也无法在邻域扩大时保持；（3）余地——代理只能加速基方法本身仍能取得的进展。将半径感知的局部泛化形式化为经典fully linear条件的推广，在固定代理类别、训练流程和基方法条件下系统测试每个因素。

Result: 在117个基准实例上：有保障的辅助（safeguarded assistance）将高精度求解数从67提升至84，而梯度替代将其降至65；从训练损失中移除梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148；跨越10个噪声水平的1000次配对比较显示不存在噪声阈值，只有提前停止的基方法。同样的因素也约束了增益上限：基于模型的信赖域求解器（余地小）在接入相同代理后从88降至86，发布的插值软件保持领先为103；在蒙特卡洛库存模型中，修复接受界面价值10.40个成本单位，而代理本身价值0.00。

Conclusion: 神经代理的价值取决于其在优化管线中的角色、适用的局部半径以及基方法可用的改进余地，而非训练曲线的拟合精度。正确的代理集成方式是"建议-审批"式辅助而非梯度替代，且需要识别基方法本身仍有提升空间时才值得接入代理。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [258] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息误差场学习（PIEFL）框架，通过在PINNs主网络达到满意精度后引入辅助误差网络，将学习目标从解场转向误差场，在不改动原网络结构的前提下，以相同的计算预算获得更高的解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在优化后期，参数更新带来的精度提升有限但计算开销持续增长，存在收益递减瓶颈，需要一种更高效的后训练优化策略来提升最终精度。

Method: PIEFL框架包含两个核心步骤：1）主网络先训练至满意精度；2）引入辅助误差网络，通过物理约束推导出误差控制方程，使误差网络学习当前近似解与精确解之间的差异，并将学到的误差修正与主网络预测相结合，从而提升最终精度。该方法无需修改主网络架构，可作为通用后训练优化策略。

Result: 在代表性PDE上的数值实验表明，PIEFL在相同计算预算下实现了更高的解精度，验证了其在提升PINNs性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFL通过将优化重心从整个解空间转移到误差修正上来，避免了持续优化全部解空间的高昂成本，为PINNs提供了一种通用、即插即用的精度提升方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [259] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEF-GT-AdamW，一种面向无线物联网边缘网络的通信高效且抗中断的分布式学习算法，结合梯度跟踪、AdamW自适应优化、双流有偏量化与误差反馈以及本地回退策略，在异构数据和不可靠无线通信条件下实现鲁棒的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线IoT边缘网络需要能在异构本地数据和通信受限链路下可靠运行的分布式学习方法，但现有分布式优化方案在严格的空口时间预算、衰落信道和丢包约束下会产生较高的通信开销和性能退化。

Method: QEF-GT-AdamW算法结合了三个关键组件：(1) 梯度跟踪以缓解非IID数据的影响；(2) AdamW自适应优化以提升训练稳定性；(3) 双流有偏量化与误差反馈以减少模型和跟踪信息交换的通信负载。同时，针对不可靠的广播通信，采用本地回退策略处理数据包丢失，并显式建模带宽、发射功率、空口约束和衰落信道对性能的影响。

Result: 在异构MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验表明，QEF-GT-AdamW在收敛性能和鲁棒性方面持续优于代表性分布式学习基线，并在有限的无线资源下实现了良好的精度-通信权衡。

Conclusion: 该算法在压缩且不可靠的无线通信条件下建立了收敛保证，验证了其在通信受限无线网络中实现高效且抗中断分布式学习的可行性。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [260] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REP-LIE是一种在微调过程中进行资源高效剪枝的新方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度估计权重重要性，引入稳定性分数解决随机性问题，实现无需完整梯度计算和全参数优化的高效模型剪枝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer预训练模型的计算和内存成本高，部署困难，尤其在资源受限环境中。传统剪枝方法依赖基于完整梯度的重要性估计，且需要先对模型进行微调才能获得满意性能，这一过程消耗大量资源。

Method: REP-LIE利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估计权重重要性，避免了完整梯度计算；引入稳定性分数以应对重要性估计中的固有随机性，作为迭代剪枝不重要模型参数的依据；剪枝后的模型通过轻量级更新进行进一步微调，无需全参数优化。

Result: 在中规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上的大量实验表明，REP-LIE相比现有方法仍能取得具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE提供了一种在微调过程中实现资源高效剪枝的有效方案，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著降低了资源消耗，为Transformer模型在资源受限环境中的部署提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [261] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: pFedMARL是一种利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）和TD3算法动态调整联邦学习（FL）聚合策略的新方法，通过服务器端和客户端双智能体协同优化，在非独立同分布（non-IID）和对抗性客户端场景下，训练半监督音频频谱Transformer模型时表现优于FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽然能保护数据隐私，但在处理异构、非独立同分布（non-IID）的客户端数据分布时表现不佳，导致全局模型次优且有偏。现有方法（如FedAvg）缺乏对客户端贡献的动态调整能力，难以在个性化与全局鲁棒性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出pFedMARL框架，采用双智能体架构：(1) 服务器端智能体基于Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)动态调整各客户端的聚合权重，以优化全局模型鲁棒性；(2) 客户端智能体在全局更新与本地更新之间进行平衡，实现无需预训练的模型个性化。

Result: 在多个非IID场景和存在对抗性客户端的情况下，pFedMARL在训练半监督音频频谱Transformer时匹配或超越了FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法，并在准确性、鲁棒性和公平性方面均有所提升。

Conclusion: pFedMARL通过MARL驱动的自适应聚合策略，能够同时提升全局模型鲁棒性和客户端个性化效果，适用于真实世界的联邦学习部署场景。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [262] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在先前基于自回归Transformer的AI干扰抑制方法基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层，以提升对结构化干扰（如数字电视OFDM信号）的抑制性能，同时保持低延迟。实验表明该方法在干扰抑制效果和推理速度上均优于传统方法及先前AI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的干扰抑制方法难以同时考虑目标信号和干扰混合信号的整体特征，而深度学习方法可联合建模两者关系从而获得更优性能。本文旨在通过FSQ分词器层和推理优化技术，在保持低延迟的同时进一步提升干扰抑制性能。

Method: 在已有的自回归Transformer模型基础上，增加一个有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层以改进干扰抑制性能；此外尝试多种推理优化技术以加速推理过程，尽可能减少精度损失。实验验证中，目标信号为数字调制的射频信号，结构化干扰为数字电视信号（一种常见OFDM传输）。

Result: 所提方法实现了低延迟并显著提升了干扰抑制性能，优于传统技术和先前基于AI的方法。通过PESQ等音频指标验证了AI方法在感知质量上的优势，并展示了算法在多种实际应用场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: FSQ分词器层的引入与推理优化技术的结合，能够在低延迟约束下有效提升结构化干扰抑制性能，并具备在实际操作场景中部署的潜力。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [263] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MSR-IVA（掩码结构残差独立向量分析）框架，用于融合sMRI与dFNC多模态数据。通过共享结构表示与状态特异性残差适应相结合，并引入掩码机制处理状态表达不完整的问题。在ADNI队列上，MSR-IVA相对基线将匹配源耦合提升6.5%，不匹配依赖降低15.7%，跨状态结构源相关性达0.9177（基线仅0.2978），实现了可控的结构共享与状态特异性适应的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合sMRI和dFNC可以揭示大脑结构与动态功能状态之间的关系，但现有方法面临两个挑战：一是同一结构潜在表示与多个状态耦合时，对每个状态独立应用IVA会得到不相关的结构分解，而强制相同分解又会抑制状态特异性关系；二是并非每个受试者都表达每个动态状态。因此需要一种状态感知的融合框架来解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出MSR-IVA（掩码结构残差IVA）框架，核心设计包括：(1) 共享结构表示，用于跨状态保持结构源的一致性；(2) 状态特异性残差适应模块，允许每个状态在共享表示基础上进行个性化调整；(3) 掩码机制，处理某些受试者在特定状态下不表达的问题。在ADNI（阿尔茨海默病神经影像计划）队列上进行验证。

Result: 在ADNI队列上，MSR-IVA相对独立的成对IVA基线将匹配源耦合提升了6.5%，将不匹配依赖降低了15.7%。对于同时表达两种状态的受试者，MSR-IVA的平均绝对跨状态结构源相关性为0.9177，而无共享基线仅为0.2978。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA实现了可控的结构共享，在保持结构源对应关系的同时允许状态特异性适应，有效解决了个体动态状态表达不完整的问题，为sMRI-dFNC多模态融合提供了更优的状态感知方案。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [264] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D^3-MOPD提出一种零开销的动态领域调度器，利用训练中已有的per-domain reverse-KL信号在线调整领域混合比例，解决MOPD中固定数据混合导致的计算浪费问题。在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型上，相比vanilla MOPD将平均师生性能差距闭合率从63%提升到97%，训练步数减少约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOPD方法在训练前固定per-domain数据混合比例，但不同领域收敛速度差异显著——部分领域早期即达到平台期，另一些则在整个训练预算中持续改进。固定混合会导致计算浪费在已收敛的领域上，同时欠训练慢收敛领域。

Method: D^3-MOPD是一个异步运行在训练进程之外的"off-process watcher"，零开销地复用训练中已生成的per-domain reverse-KL信号。该watcher周期性跟踪每个领域的KL轨迹，估计剩余提升空间和当前改善速率，据此在线调整领域采样比例，且不修改核心训练循环。该方法可自然扩展到任意数量的领域。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型上，从四个领域专家教师蒸馏时，D^3-MOPD将平均师生性能差距闭合率从vanilla MOPD的63%提升至97%；达成相同峰值性能时rollout步数减少约3倍；且在七个基准中三个超越专家教师。

Conclusion: 通过动态调度领域混合比例，D^3-MOPD在零额外开销的情况下显著提升了多教师蒸馏的效率与性能，且领域数量越多、收敛模式越多样，调度器的收益越大。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts. Existing approaches typically fix the per-domain data mixture before training, overlooking the fact that different domains converge at substantially different rates: some plateau early while others continue to improve throughout the training budget. A fixed mixture therefore wastes compute on fast-converging domains and undertrains slower-converging ones. To address this, we propose D$^3$-MOPD (Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD), a zero-overhead scheduler that repurposes the per-domain reverse-KL signal already produced during training to adapt the domain mixture online. Running asynchronously outside the training process, an off-process watcher periodically tracks each domain's KL trajectory, estimates remaining headroom and current improvement rate, and accordingly adjusts the domain sampling ratios without altering the core training loop. Our D$^3$-MOPD scales naturally to arbitrary numbers of domains, and the expected benefit grows as more domains introduce more diverse convergence patterns for the scheduler to exploit. On a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B student distilled from four domain-expert teachers, D$^3$-MOPD closes 97% of the average student-to-teacher performance gap, compared with 63% for vanilla MOPD, reaches the same peak performance with an approximately 3$\times$ reduction in rollout steps, and surpasses the specialist teachers on three of seven benchmarks.

</details>


### [265] [Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction for Long-Horizon Prediction in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25017)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR)方法，通过在训练期间让潜在世界模型自由运行（与评估时完全相同），并对每个潜变量解码后的重建误差进行惩罚，弥合了训练（仅解码锚定于观测的潜变量）与评估（在模型自身自由运行输出上解码）之间的分布差距。该方法不增加参数，仅增加训练计算成本，在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上将有效预测时间从3.87提高到6.97时间单位（1.80倍改进），并在10/10预注册配置中验证了改进的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型存在训练与部署之间的分布偏移问题：训练时解码器仅对锚定于真实观测的潜变量进行解码，而在部署时却需要对模型自身自由滚动数百步后产生的潜变量进行解码，两者分布不一致导致性能下降。作者旨在通过最小化这一训练-部署差距来提升模型的预测能力。

Method: RDR引入单一损失项：在训练过程中让模型自由滚动（与评估完全一致），对所有滚动潜变量进行解码，并将其重建误差与真实ground truth进行对比惩罚。该方法无需额外参数，仅增加训练时计算开销，且权重为零时退化为标准目标函数，使得所有对比实验均为单开关A/B测试。

Result: 在混沌Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上，RDR将有效预测时间从3.87±0.23提高到6.97±0.42时间单位（1.80×改进），在相同193,568参数下实现；该改进在未参与选择的种子上得到证实，并在10/10预注册配置中以1.71-2.50×的比例保持。结果来自单一系统；潜在宽度增加时优势增大的扫描是描述性发现，两个经典任务的对照实验为初步结果。

Conclusion: RDR通过简单的训练时自由运行重建损失有效缩小了潜在世界模型的训练-评估分布差距，在混沌动力系统预测上取得了显著的性能提升。作者对该主要结果保持审慎态度——目前仅在一个系统上得到验证，宽度效应扫描为描述性结论，经典任务的对照实验属于初步阶段，暗示需要进一步更广泛的验证。

Abstract: A latent world model trains its decoder on latents anchored to observations, then deploys it on the model's own free-running rollout, hundreds of steps past the last observation. Rollout-Decoded Reconstruction (RDR) closes this gap with a single loss term that free-runs the model during training exactly as evaluation will, decodes every rollout latent, and penalizes reconstruction error against ground truth. The term adds no parameters, costs training-time compute only, and reduces to the standard objective at weight zero, so every comparison in this paper is a one-flag A/B. On the chaotic Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equation, RDR raises valid prediction time (the time to first crossing of normalized error 0.5) from $3.87 \pm 0.23$ to $6.97 \pm 0.42$ time units at an identical 193,568 parameters, a $1.80\times$ improvement confirmed on seeds never used in selection and holding in 10 of 10 preregistered configurations at ratios of 1.71-2.50$\times$. The results come from a single system; a sweep in which the advantage grows with latent width is descriptive, and control experiments on two classic tasks are preliminary.

</details>


### [266] [On the Representational Geometry of Dynamic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25034)
*Richard F. M. Lim,Ruriko Yoshida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度研究了标准神经架构在动态规划（DP）任务上无法泛化到更长输入的问题。作者证明每个有限min-plus DP等价于DAG上的最短路径问题，并进一步等价于一个热带多项式，其扩展Newton多面体编码了路径获胜的决策边界。文中证明了图、多项式、多面体三种描述在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面构成同构的半环，并通过操作刻画了所有结构冗余。针对长度泛化问题，作者给出了两个结构性否定结果：半环的两种降维操作（将变量设为恒等元）既不单射也非封闭；级联和并联组合无法从更小子DAG构造所有DAG拓扑，即使所有仅含终端的操作也无法捕获所有DP组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经架构在DP类任务上无法从短输入泛化到长输入，作者希望从几何角度理解这一泛化困难的根本原因。通过将DP问题映射为图、热带多项式和Newton多面体的等价描述，试图在几何层面刻画决策边界的结构，从而解释长度泛化失败的本质。

Method: 将每个有限min-plus DP问题转化为DAG上的最短路径问题，再等价表示为热带多项式，利用扩展Newton多面体编码决策边界。在形式多项式和计算函数两个层面证明图、多项式、多面体三种描述构成同构半环，并用操作刻画结构冗余。针对长度泛化，分析决策边界在长度T与T+1之间的保持性，考察半环降维操作的单射性和封闭性，以及级联/并联组合对DAG拓扑的构造能力。

Result: 证明了图、多项式、多面体三者构成同构半环，建立了两个层级的等价性。给出两个结构性否定结果：(1) 半环的两种降维方式（将变量设为恒等元）既不单射，也不能保证在DP内封闭；(2) 级联和并联组合无法从更小的子DAG构造所有DAG拓扑，即使加入所有仅终端的操作也不能捕获全部DP组合。

Conclusion: 长度泛化的困难具有深层几何结构根源：决策边界在长度扩展时无法通过半环运算保持或构造，DP组合的结构冗余导致降维操作不封闭。这些否定结果解释了为何标准神经架构难以在DP任务上实现长度泛化，为设计更好的架构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Standard neural architectures often fail to generalize to longer inputs for dynamic programming (DP) targets. We investigate what makes this hard geometrically. Every finite min-plus DP is a shortest path on a DAG, which is equivalently a tropical polynomial whose extended Newton polyhedron encodes the decision boundary of which path wins. We prove these three descriptions (graph, polynomial, polyhedron) form isomorphic semirings at two levels --- formal polynomials and their computed functions --- connected by operations that characterize all structural redundancies. We then address the length-generalization question geometrically: does the decision boundary at length $T$ decide the boundary at $T+1$? We present two structural negatives. The semiring's two native ways to reduce dimension (setting a variable to each identity) are neither injective nor always closed within the DP. Series and parallel composition fail to construct all DAG topologies from smaller sub-DAGs, and even all terminal-only operations do not capture all DP compositions.

</details>


### [267] [DeMMO: Longitudinal and Cross-Disease Modelling of Digital Mobility Outcomes via Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25073)
*Menghui Zhou,Zhipeng Yuan,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeMMO，一个可解释的纵向多疾病多结局学习框架，用于基于可穿戴传感器数字移动性指标（DMOs）监测多种疾病进展。该框架通过自动跨疾病跨结局关系学习机制，在不共享参与者的疾病队列间实现选择性信息共享，并在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集上优于九个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DMO研究大多只针对单一疾病、单一时间点，无法建模多变量DMO关系如何跨疾病联合演变。虽然已有时间多任务框架可对单一疾病建模进展，但不能跨疾病联合建模多个预测结局，尤其当疾病队列不共享参与者时。这限制了DMO在临床监测中的广泛应用。

Method: DeMMO将每个疾病-结局目标表示为纵向DMO系数矩阵，结合时间正则化与稳定及访视特异性特征选择。核心技术贡献是自动跨疾病和跨结局关系学习机制，直接从纵向映射中学习带符号的关系，实现无需配对参与者的选择性信息共享。

Result: 在Mobilise-D大规模多中心数据集（24个协调的真实世界DMO，5次访视，多种活动受限疾病）上评估，DeMMO在整体及结局特异的预测性能上超越九个强线性/纵向/深度回归基线。稳定性选择识别出可靠的纵向DMO模式，可服务于后续临床验证。

Conclusion: DeMMO成功填补了DMO研究中跨疾病、跨结局纵向联合建模的空白，通过可解释的关系学习机制实现高效的跨队列信息共享，为多疾病移动能力监测提供了可靠的预测与特征选择工具。

Abstract: Digital mobility outcomes (DMOs) derived from wearable sensors characterise mobility in daily life and offer a promising means of monitoring disease progression. Yet most DMO studies examine one disease at one visit; they do not model how multivariate DMO relationships with multiple clinical outcomes evolve jointly across diseases. Technically, existing temporal multi-task frameworks can model progression within an individual disease, but they do not jointly model multiple prediction outcomes across diseases, particularly when disease cohorts do not share participants. To address these gaps, we propose DeMMO, an interpretable framework for longitudinal, multi-disease, and multi-outcome learning. DeMMO represents each disease-outcome objective by a longitudinal DMO coefficient matrix and combines temporal regularisation with stable and visit-specific feature selection. Its central technical contribution is an automatic cross-disease and cross-outcome relation-learning mechanism that learns signed relations directly from these longitudinal mappings, enabling selective information sharing without paired participants. We evaluate DeMMO on the recently released, large-scale, multicentre Mobilise-D dataset, which provides a new opportunity to study 24 harmonised real-world DMOs over five visits across multiple mobility-limiting conditions. Against nine strong linear, longitudinal, and deep-regression baselines, DeMMO achieves the best overall and outcome-specific prediction performance, with significant improvements over the strongest baselines. Stability selection further identifies reliable longitudinal DMO patterns for subsequent clinical validation and disease monitoring. The implementation code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/menghui-zhou/DeMMO.

</details>


### [268] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过语义流将每个预测水平归因于时间相关的历史滞后，生成滞后-水平归因矩阵，并使用稀疏局部替代模型产生人类可读的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型的后置解释方法常常忽略时序依赖性，且无法提供针对特定预测水平的解释，导致高 stakes 场景下模型预测难以被理解。

Method: 将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流量化信息在模型内部表示中的时序演化；通过聚合语义流构建滞后-水平归因矩阵；生成保持结构的扰动并拟合稀疏局部替代模型，产生可读且时序连贯的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，语义流变体在忠实度上达到或优于标准后置基线方法，同时计算效率显著更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别了需要谨慎应用解释的场景。

Conclusion: 语义流框架是一种高效、忠实且稳健的时间序列预测可解释性方法，能够提供水平分解的时序归因解释，并指出其适用边界。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [269] [The Frame Kernel Method for Multiscale Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25084)
*Branden Frieden,Ryan Whitehead,M. Keith Ballard,Robert M. Kirby,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种原生多尺度算子学习方法，用于多尺度偏微分方程（PDE）数值求解器的代理建模。核心创新在于一种新的多尺度核框架函数逼近技术，将算子学习问题转化为学习输出函数框架系数与输入函数框架系数之间的映射关系，从而在泛化时自动实现输出函数的多尺度分解。该方法适用于张量积网格和点云，并提供了插值证明、误差估计和数值收敛率。在文献中具有挑战性的多尺度PDE问题上，该方法比流行的神经算子显著更准确，同时支持后验多尺度分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多尺度PDE的求解通常计算代价高昂，而现有的神经算子方法在捕捉多尺度特征方面精度不足，且难以提供可解释的多尺度分解。作者旨在开发一种既能高效代理多尺度PDE数值求解器，又能在泛化时自动提供多尺度分解信息的算子学习方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的多尺度核框架函数逼近技术，将算子学习重新表述为学习输入与输出函数的框架系数之间的映射。利用核框架结构，泛化步骤自动产生输出函数的多尺度分解。方法同时支持张量积网格与点云两种离散形式，并伴随严格的插值证明和误差估计。

Result: 在文献中具有挑战性的多尺度PDE代理建模问题上，该多尺度核框架方法相比流行的神经算子方法精度显著更高，且模型输出支持后验多尺度分解。数值实验验证了所提框架逼近的收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种兼具理论保障和实用性能的替代性算子学习范式：不仅能以更高精度代理多尺度PDE求解器，还天然具备多尺度可解释性，为多尺度科学计算中的代理建模提供了新的有力工具。

Abstract: We present a natively multiscale operator learning method for the surrogate modeling of (numerical solvers for) multiscale partial differential equations (PDEs). The primary novelty of our method lies in a novel multiscale kernel frame function approximation technique. Leveraging this new kernel frame technique, we cast the operator learning problem as one of learning frame coefficients of output functions as a function of frame coefficients of input functions. The generalization step then automatically allows for a multiscale decomposition of the output functions. Our method is applicable to both tensor-product grids and point clouds. We present interpolation proofs, error estimates, and numerical convergence rates for our frame approximation. We the demonstrate the applicability of our method for the surrogate modeling of inherently multiscale PDEs. The new multiscale frame kernel method is significantly more accurate than popular neural operators on challenging problems from the literature, while simultaneously admitting an a posteriori multiscale decomposition upon generalization.

</details>
