We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [400] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Somgyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProgRouter是一个在线进度引导的多智能体LLM路由框架，通过多视角任务进度评估和双路径进度预测机制，在每一步工作流中自适应选择最优LLM代理，在保持任务求解质量的同时显著降低运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM工作流虽然擅长解决复杂开放任务，但重复的LLM调用和长上下文累积导致高昂运营成本。现有级联路由方法仅做一次性、查询级别的决策，无法适应多步骤工作流中动态且状态依赖的特性——每一步应选择哪个LLM取决于当前的任务进展、剩余难度和成本效率需求。

Method: ProgRouter包含三个核心组件：(1) 多视角任务进度评分器，结合粗粒度的工作流结果状态和细粒度的子任务完成度、进度趋势及工作流状态质量信号；(2) 双路径任务进度预测器，用于估计每个候选LLM的进度增益；(3) 自适应元门控机制，在线做出逐步路由决策，平衡进度增益、任务时间预算和长期运营成本效率。

Result: 在HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500和ASQA四个基准上，涵盖智能体代码生成、数学推理和检索增强长形式问答任务，ProgRouter相比关键基线方法在保持强任务求解性能的同时显著降低了运营成本。

Conclusion: ProgRouter验证了在线、进度感知的逐步路由策略能够有效解决多智能体LLM工作流中的成本-质量权衡问题，为多步骤智能体系统中的自适应模型选择提供了新的方法论方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [401] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsymSpec 提出非对称投机解码框架，让小模型草稿器读取完整上下文，而大模型验证器仅处理压缩视图，通过对比δ-融合与发散感知接受门控，在约90%满精度下实现1.3–1.7×吞吐加速和0.2–0.3×计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM流水线中上下文累积导致推理成本激增，部署中常用输入压缩来控制延迟但会降低任务精度。现有投机解码(SD)假设草稿器与验证器共享相同上下文，无法解决精度与开销之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出AsymSpec非对称投机解码框架：轻量草稿器读全量输入，大型验证器操作压缩视图；通过对比δ-融合(contrastive δ-fusion)将草稿器logits引导至验证器，并由发散感知接受门控(divergence-aware acceptance gate)保持验证稳定性与高草稿接受率。

Result: 在四种agentic能力与两个端到端agent基准上，AsymSpec平均达到全上下文精度的约90%，在隔离文本能力上实现1.3–1.7×吞吐量加速，计算成本仅为0.2–0.3×。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，非对称上下文访问在压缩丢弃关键推理信号时能带来显著收益，为agentic LLM部署中的精度-开销权衡提供了新解法。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [402] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动定理证明中的逐步证明构造过程建模为形式演算诱导的转换系统中的策略，为clausal connection tableaux（子句连接表）统一了leanCoP风格的搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格的规划接口，使策略学习方法可直接应用。配备图神经网络对证明编辑进行评分，通过模仿学习训练，并系统评估在不同搜索脚手架程度下（从完整符号回溯到纯网络驱动）的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明器需要逐步构建证明，每一步都要决定添加或删除什么内容。现有方法（如leanCoP的搜索与plCoP/rlCoP的规划）缺乏统一的策略学习框架，难以直接将强化学习/模仿学习等策略学习方法应用于证明构造过程。

Method: 1) 将证明构造建模为形式演算（子句连接tableaux）诱导的转换系统中的策略；2) 将leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划统一为同一接口上的有状态策略；3) 使用图神经网络对证明编辑进行评分，利用跨问题可迁移的结构信息；4) 通过模仿学习从已有证明中训练；5) 系统评估去除搜索脚手架（从完整符号回溯到纯网络驱动策略）后的性能保持情况。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1数据集上，在固定步骤预算内，学习策略比leanCoP多解决了46%的问题，且达到证明所需的步骤数少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 将证明构造统一为转换系统中的策略框架是有效的，图神经网络与模仿学习相结合的学习策略显著优于传统符号搜索方法，且大幅减少了证明搜索步骤。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [403] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace Integrity（轨迹完整性）作为LLM数据代理的部署可靠性评估标准，指出仅依赖答案准确度不足以保证系统可靠性——无效的计算轨迹也可能产生基准正确的答案。通过执行契约（execution contracts）操作化该标准，并引入CAIT Rate衡量"答案正确但轨迹无效"的静默失败风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据任务中，LLM数据代理可能通过无效的计算轨迹产生基准正确的答案，导致答案准确度无法作为足够的可靠性信号。自然语言推理和自由形式的理由不能可靠地指定真实世界系统所需的算子级程序，这种"结构鸿沟"（Structure Gap）使得仅评估答案的范式存在部署隐患。

Method: 提出Trace Integrity框架，包含六个评估维度：显式性、可执行性、模式有效性、算子忠实性、可重放性和可审计性。通过执行契约将用户意图绑定到模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案链接。引入CAIT（Correct Answer / Invalid Trace）Rate指标，衡量仅依赖答案评估时错误计为成功输出的比例。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev基准上，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL三种方法的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，但其Trace Integrity通过率分别为39%、43%和40%，CAIT Rate分别高达55%、59.1%和45.8%。结果表明答案准确度、轨迹有效性和静默失败风险是三个相互独立且差异显著的评估信号。

Conclusion: 真实世界的LLM数据代理不应仅根据输出是否匹配参考答案来评估，而应评估其输出是否基于可审计的计算过程。Trace Integrity为部署提供了比答案准确度更可靠的评估标准。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [404] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型智能体能否在分布式环境中自组织形成技术社会。通过SwarmWorld模拟器，同质LLM智能体在无预设角色和配方的条件下自主构建持久化技术制品，其社会性技术组合优于独立搜索基线，智能体分化为探索、建造、维护和协调等行为，并通过物理stigmergy实现文化积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统大多依赖直接对话、预设角色或中心化工作流，尚未验证去中心化同质智能体能否通过共享环境自主涌现集体智能和功能性技术。

Method: 提出SwarmWorld环境，将认知与后果分离：智能体在固定动作和材料图式内提出架构与控制器，由确定性模拟器在不可见扰动下评估功能。同质LLM智能体在空间中探索、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久化制品并编写可执行控制器。

Result: 共享社会比强best-of-N隔离搜索基线发展出更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合，但隔离搜索在最强单个制品上仍具竞争力。智能体分化为探索、建造、维护、协调行为并随世界成熟过渡；技术通过协作构建、可执行继承和持久智能体-制品网络积累，大多数复用始于物理观察而非通信。

Conclusion: 显式文化机制能放大协作与组织但功能收益取决于结果与时间尺度；物理stigmergy单独即可支撑有能力的社 会，而交互驱动持久技术生态而非普遍更优的个体发明。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [405] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行星预测引擎（PPE），一个端到端的自主AI系统，可直接从自然语言查询出发完成多模态数据检索、融合和模型选择，在多个地理空间预测任务中显著超越SOTA和专家调优基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对粮食安全、灾害风险、疾病暴发等全球挑战需要高保真地理空间建模，但当前预测模型的构建受限于碎片化的数据生态系统，需要人工进行数据检索、多模态数据整理融合及反复的模型选择，技术门槛高且效率低下。

Method: PPE是一个自主AI系统，通过自然语言查询驱动端到端工作流。它实时综合多模态数据集，从开放网络和地球观测平台（Data Commons、Google Earth Engine）检索时空相关的协变量，并与地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）融合；同时自动搜索任务定制的模型架构族，并配备自动化过拟合防护机制。

Result: 在美国空间回归任务中，PPE在21个CDC健康指标（R² 76.8% vs 60.0%）、FEMA国家风险指数（64.9% vs 60.0%）和Social Vulnerability Index（66.2% vs 58.6%）上均优于基线；在数据稀缺的尼日利亚粮食安全指标降尺度任务中准确率翻倍（R² 66.1% vs 31.5%）；在2026年刚果（金）Bundibugyo埃博拉疫情预测中Recall@10达83.3%（识别出18个新入侵卫生区中的15个），较公开SOTA提升10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: PPE通过将自主多模态行星数据发现与目标化模型优化相结合，降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，能够实现快速、定制化、专家级水平的部署。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [406] [Probabilistic Performance Analysis of Parallel Signature Search Strategies in Multi-Level Tree Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25087)
*Jingwei Li,Thomas G. Robertazzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概率框架，用于在读取任何文件之前预测五种层次化分布式搜索策略（从逐层扫描到全树并行）的完成时间。节点扫描时间用签名存在性的混合分布建模，层时间用次序统计量建模，并行子树扫描用极值论证建模，并结合生成函数处理容量约束下的占用问题。每个性能公式都带有精确性标签（精确、插入式、渐近或有界），并通过蒙特卡洛模拟量化近似误差及识别适用范围。多核原型验证了全树/层/子树级并行之间的粗粒度分离，但发现同步开销会抹平相近策略间的预测差异。整个框架可在亚毫秒内完成设计评估，支持后续资源成本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化分布式搜索（包括分布式索引遍历、深度包检测和序列比对）在实践中广泛存在，但搜索者需要在不同并行策略间做选择（逐层扫描、子树内扇出或全树同时启动）。现有分析仅部分回答了这一问题：它们用签名持有文件的统计量来描述每个节点，对多签名文件需要运行时才能得知的量，无法在搜索开始前给出先验预测。

Method: 构建了一个概率框架，其中：节点扫描时间建模为基于签名存在性的混合分布；层时间用次序统计量建模；并行子树扫描借助极值理论；已知签名计数时，容量约束下的占用问题用生成函数求解。每个性能公式带有精确性标签（精确/精确-in-regime、插入式、渐近、有界），并用蒙特卡洛模拟量化近似误差并识别其适用域。实现了一个多核原型验证理论预测。

Result: 多核原型复现了全树并行、层级并行和子树级并行之间的粗粒度性能分离，但也显示同步开销可能消除相近策略之间的预测差异。框架能在亚毫秒内给出设计示例的先验完成时间预测，计算开销可忽略，并具有显式的精度适用范围。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了可解释精度域且计算代价极低的先验完成时间预测，能够替代靠运行时信息才能做出的分析，并可进一步支撑资源成本优化决策。

Abstract: Hierarchical distributed search, locating a data pattern, or signature, across a tree-structured collection of files, underlies distributed index traversal, deep packet inspection and sequence alignment. A practitioner must decide how much parallelism to employ: scan each layer sequentially, fan out within subtrees, or launch the whole tree at once. Existing analyses answer this only partially: they characterize every node by the statistics of a signature-holding file and, for multi-signature files, need quantities revealed only at run time. We develop a probabilistic framework predicting the completion time of five search strategies, spanning sequential to full-tree parallelism, before any file is read. Node scan times are modeled as a mixture over signature presence, layer times as order statistics, and parallel subtree scans by extreme-value arguments; when signature counts are known, occupancy under capacity constraints is treated by generating functions. Each performance formula carries an exactness label: exact (or exact-in-regime), plug-in, asymptotic or bound, with each approximation quantified against Monte Carlo simulation and its regime identified. A multicore prototype reproduces the coarse separation between full-tree, layer- and subtree-level parallelism, but shows that synchronization overhead can erase the predicted separation between close strategies. The framework delivers a priori completion-time predictions with explicit accuracy regimes and negligible computational cost, the design example evaluated in under a millisecond; these timing models can support subsequent resource-cost optimization.

</details>


### [407] [Hierarchical Shared Memory-Aware Optimization for TRSM on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25469)
*Xinzhe Chen,Haowei Li,Lijuan Hu,Wenjing Ma,Fangfang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMA-TRSM，一个面向左侧下三角TRSM的分层共享内存感知优化框架，在NVIDIA A100、H800和Hygon DCU Z100上分别比cuBLAS和rocBLAS实现最高2.05倍和2.06倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRSM是BLAS Level-3的基础操作，支撑LU/Cholesky分解、稀疏直接求解器和矩阵求逆等关键计算。左侧下三角情形下，前代替换引入严格的按行依赖，且共享内存不足以同时容纳宽数据类型（如双精度复数）的两个操作数矩阵，导致GPU上的高效实现极具挑战性。

Method: 针对小规模（m,n<=64），通过循环展开和指令重排实现流水线计算-访存重叠，并设计双线程组七级流水线策略应对双精度复数的共享内存限制；针对大规模问题，提出对角块解耦优化，将对角块求逆的共享内存占用降至O(IB)，并基于矩阵规模和硬件特性自适应选择块大小；采用离线性能剖析与在线查找的编译期配置选择框架，实现零运行时开销的最优块大小选择。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100、H800和Hygon DCU Z100上，HSMA-TRSM相较cuBLAS达到最高2.05倍加速比，相较rocBLAS达到最高2.06倍加速比。加速效果在共享内存受限的双精度复数小规模场景以及采用自适应分块优化GEMM主导更新的大规模实数场景中最为显著。

Conclusion: HSMA-TRSM通过分层共享内存感知的优化策略有效解决了TRSM在GPU上的性能瓶颈，证明了针对不同规模尺度和数据类型量身定制的优化方案的必要性；同时也指出在某些场景下成熟的厂商内核已无太多优化空间，验证了优化工作的合理边界。

Abstract: Triangular Solve with Multiple Right-hand Sides (TRSM) is a fundamental BLAS Level-3 operation that underpins LU/Cholesky decomposition, sparse direct solvers, and matrix inversion. In the left-side lower-triangular case studied in this paper, efficient GPU implementation remains challenging because forward substitution introduces strict row-wise dependencies, and shared memory is too scarce to hold both operand matrices for wide data types such as double complex. This paper presents HSMA-TRSM, a hierarchical shared memory-aware optimization framework for left-side lower-triangular TRSM on NVIDIA A100, NVIDIA H800, and Hygon DCU Z100 accelerators. For the small-scale regime (m,n<=64), we design a pipelined compute-memory overlap mechanism through loop unrolling and instruction reordering, and propose a dual thread-group seven-stage pipeline strategy to address shared memory constraints for double complex types. For large-scale problems, we introduce a diagonal block decoupling optimization with an O(IB)shared-memory footprint for diagonal block inversion, enabling adaptive block size selection based on matrix scale and hardware characteristics. A compile-time configuration selection framework based on offline profiling and online lookup selects the optimal block size per platform with zero runtime overhead. Evaluated on NVIDIA A100, H800, and Hygon DCU Z100, HSMA-TRSM achieves peak speedups of 2.05xover cuBLAS and 2.06xover rocBLAS. The gains are strongest in shared-memory-constrained double-complex small cases and in large real-type cases where adaptive blocking improves GEMM-dominated updates, while mature vendor kernels leave less optimization headroom in some regimes.

</details>


### [408] [psRL: Efficient Training for Agentic AI via Training-Time Prefix Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25683)
*Mianjie Yu,Zizhao Mo,Huanyu Qu,Zhirong Qian,Huanle Xu,Cen Li,Zifeng Zhao,Zhi Zhou,Jinhua Zhou,Jun Xie,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: psRL是一个面向agentic AI强化学习训练的新系统，利用训练样本间的前缀冗余性，通过前缀共享与KV缓存管理，在分布式训练中实现高效的负载均衡和内存管理，较现有系统吞吐量提升最高5.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代agentic AI训练中，系统瓶颈正从rollout阶段转向update阶段。树状结构和逐步强化学习等新兴采样策略大幅增加了训练样本量，但边际rollout成本较低，导致update阶段主导端到端执行时间。同时，生产环境trace显示训练样本间存在大量前缀冗余，这为优化提供了新的机会。

Method: psRL利用update阶段固有的全局可见性和数据不可变性，设计了两种新颖的前缀共享机制，实现跨GPU worker的灵活细粒度工作负载分配，同时优化前缀复用和负载均衡；并实现了新的底层KV缓存管理器，支持可适应的块大小分配和动态KV缓存，最大化内存利用率并保持高前缀命中率。

Result: 使用生产环境trace的评估表明，psRL在吞吐量上相比现有系统最高提升5.2倍。

Conclusion: psRL有效解决了agentic AI训练中update阶段的前缀冗余问题，通过前缀共享和优化的KV缓存管理显著提升了分布式训练系统的吞吐性能，源码即将公开发布。

Abstract: In modern agentic AI training, the system bottleneck is shifting from rollout to update. Emerging sampling strategies such as tree-structured and step-wise RL greatly increase training sample volume while incurring relatively low marginal rollout cost, causing the update phase to dominate the end-to-end execution time. Crucially, this shift exposes a new optimization opportunity, as production traces reveal substantial prefix redundancy across training samples. In this paper, we propose psRL (prefix sharing for RL), a new training system for agentic AI designed to exploit prefix redundancy among training samples. Leveraging the global visibility and data immutability inherent to the update phase, psRL achieves efficient workload scheduling and memory management for distributed training. Specifically, psRL introduces two novel prefix-sharing mechanisms that enable flexible, fine-grained workload distribution across GPU workers, simultaneously optimizing prefix reuse and achieving load balancing. Moreover, psRL implements a new underlying KV cache manager that facilitates adaptable block-size allocation and dynamic KV caching, maximizing memory utilization while maintaining a high prefix hit rate. Evaluations using production traces demonstrate that psRL outperforms existing systems by up to 5.2x in throughput. The source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>


### [409] [An Oversubscription and Service Pricing Exploitation-Based Profit Maximization Framework for Industry Cloud Resource Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25712)
*Deepika Saxena,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向行业云提供商的资源管理框架，通过资源超订和异构服务定价模型来最大化盈利和运营效率。框架采用自适应集成机器学习预测模型预估VM资源利用率，利用模糊C均值聚类将资源使用相似的用户分组，并结合延时敏感模型和尽力而为模型两种定价策略来提升收益、降低电力成本。实验结果表明该框架显著降低了电费（55.56%）、功率消耗（60.7%）和活跃服务器数量（51%），同时将资源利用率和利润分别提升了60%和51.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业云提供商面临资源浪费和运营成本高企的问题，传统资源管理方式未能充分利用资源超订带来的收益潜力，同时也缺乏有效的定价策略来平衡用户需求与盈利能力。

Method: 该框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 自适应集成机器学习驱动的预测模型，基于用户VM的历史资源利用数据进行前瞻性资源使用预估；(2) 模糊C均值聚类算法，将具有相似预测资源使用的VM分组，以确定部署所需的VM数量和配置；(3) 双类别云服务定价模型（延时敏感模型和尽力而为模型），用于对用户请求进行分类和执行VM选择，以最大化收入和降低数据中心电力成本。

Result: 在两个基准VM轨迹数据上的实验和与最先进方法的对比验证了框架性能：电费账单降低55.56%，功率消耗降低最多60.7%，活跃服务器数量减少最多51%，同时资源利用率提升最多60%，利润提升最多51.18%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架通过资源超订、智能预测分组和异构定价策略的有机结合，能够有效提升行业云提供商的盈利能力和运营效率，同时大幅降低数据中心的能源相关成本。

Abstract: This article proposed a novel industry cloud resource management framework that exploits resource oversubscription and heterogeneous service pricing models to maximize profitability and operational efficiency for industry cloud providers. The framework proposes an adaptive ensemble machine learning driven prediction model for proactive estimation of resource utilization of Virtual Machines (VM)s based on previous resource utilization of respective users' VMs to minimize resource wastage due to oversubscription by them. Accordingly, the VMs having similar predicted resource usage are grouped using Fuzzy C means clustering. This helps to determine the required number of VMs with specific configuration to be deployed before executing user requests. Concurrently, the framework incorporates two distinct categories of cloud service pricing models, namely the Delay Sensitive Model and the Best-Effort Model. Accordingly, the user requests are classified and executed by selecting the most suitable VMs, with the goal of maximizing revenue and reducing electricity costs in cloud data centers (CDCs). Experimental simulation and comparison against state-of-the-art methods, using two benchmark VM traces, validates the performance of proposed framework. It significantly reduces electricity bills by 55.56 percentage, power consumption and active servers by up to 60.7 percentage and 51 percentage, respectively, while improving resource utilization and profits by up to 60 percentage and 51.18 percentage, respectively

</details>


### [410] [A Spatially-Aware Publish-Subscribe Middleware for IoT Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25728)
*Philipp Ungrund,Kurt Rothermel,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MQTT5协议扩展主题发布-订阅模型的方法，实现了空间过滤与路由能力，使CPS/IoT系统无需修改客户端即可支持空间感知的订阅与消息分发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间和邻近感知是CPS和IoT中高效通信的关键，但现有中间件缺乏对空间感知的标准化支持，严重限制了位置相关应用的普及。

Method: 采用灵活的几何方法描述空间过滤器，扩展主题发布-订阅模型；通过几何世界模型与增强的主题代理协作，在MQTT5协议框架内实现空间约束的消息分发，且不违反标准或修改客户端。

Result: 基于合成和真实数据的实验表明，系统效率高、代理端计算开销极小，且在实际IoT设备上部署可行。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不改变现有MQTT5标准兼容性的前提下，为IoT/CPS系统提供了高效、可部署的空间感知消息分发机制。

Abstract: Spatial and proximity awareness are critical enablers for efficient communication in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) and the Internet of Things (IoT). However, the lack of suitable middleware support and standardized mechanisms for spatial awareness significantly limits the pervasiveness of location-dependent applications. In this paper, we present a novel approach that extends topic-based publish-subscribe, a dominant messaging model in this domain, with integrated spatial filtering and routing capabilities. Our approach leverages a highly flexible geometric method for describing spatial filters, enabling expressive and efficient spatial subscriptions. A geometric world model operates together with an enhanced topic-based broker to realize spatially constrained message dissemination. Importantly, this functionality is achieved using the MQTT5 protocol without violating the standard or requiring any modifications to existing client implementations. Experimental results based on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate strong system efficiency, minimal computational overhead at the broker, and the practical feasibility of deploying the approach on existing IoT devices.

</details>


### [411] [REE-TM: Reliable and Energy-Efficient Traffic Management Model for Diverse Cloud Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25747)
*Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Deepika Saxena,Volker Lindenstruth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出REE-TM（可靠且能效的流量管理）方法，利用互联网工作负载的异质性特征进行资源分配，通过TG-QNN（Toffoli门量子神经网络）工作负载估计器和QBHO（量子黑洞）算法优化，实现高效资源管理与拥塞检测，相比无REE-TM方案可靠性提升30.25%，能效提升23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云计算资源管理文献对来自互联网用户多样化作业请求的异构特征考虑不足或彻底忽视，而工作负载需求的多样性对云服务的资源分配和管理效率有关键影响，因此需要一种能够充分利用流量异构性的管理方法。

Method: 该方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 作业分类机制，将异构作业请求按其资源需求和预期复杂度进行分类；(2) 选择最优的虚拟节点和物理节点组合来执行分类后的作业；(3) 提出Toffoli门量子神经网络（TG-QNN）作为新颖的工作负载估计器，通过量子黑洞优化算法（QBHO）实现学习过程中的互连权重优化。基于预估的工作负载计算即将到来互联网流量的熵，结合多种流量状态分析来检测可能的资源拥塞。

Result: 通过基准数据集进行仿真评估，与最优版本及无REE-TM版本进行比较，结果表明REE-TM在关键指标上表现有效：相比无REE-TM方案，可靠性提升高达30.25%，能效提升高达23%。

Conclusion: REE-TM通过有效利用互联网流量的异构特征，结合量子计算启发的预测模型和优化算法，在保障云服务可靠性的同时显著提升了能源效率，验证了其在云资源管理中处理多样化工作负载的有效性。

Abstract: Diversity of workload demands lays a critical impact on efficient resource allocation and management of cloud services. The existing literature has either weakly considered or overlooked the heterogeneous feature of job requests received from wide range of internet services users. To address this context, the proposed approach named Reliable and Energy Efficient Traffic Management (REE-TM) has exploited the diversity of internet traffic in terms of variation in resource demands and expected complexity. Specifically, REE-TM incorporates categorization of heterogeneous job requests and executes them by selecting the most admissible virtual node (a software-defined instance such as a virtual machine or container) and physical node (an actual hardware server or compute host) within the cloud infrastructure. To deal with resource-contention-based resource failures and performance degradation, a novel workload estimator 'Toffoli Gate-based Quantum Neural Network' (TG-QNN) is proposed, wherein learning process or interconnection weights optimization is achieved using Quantum version of BlackHole (QBHO) algorithm. The proactively estimated workload is used to compute entropy of the upcoming internet traffic with various traffic states analysis for detection of probable resource-congestion. REE-TM is extensively evaluated through simulations using a benchmark dataset and compared with optimal and without REE-TM versions. The performance evaluation and comparison of REE-TM with measured significant metrics reveal its effectiveness in assuring higher reliability by up to 30.25% and energy-efficiency by up to 23% as compared without REE-TM.

</details>


### [412] [Quantum Blackhole Learning-Optimized Hadamard Neural Network Model for Dynamic Resource Reservation in Industry Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25754)
*Deepika Saxena,Hari Mohan Gaur,Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Anand Mohan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子黑洞优化的哈达玛神经网络（QB-HNN）模型，用于工业云的高精度工作负载预测和主动资源预留。该模型通过哈达玛门激活函数的量子比特神经元网络实现模式学习，并引入量子黑洞双相位优化算法（QB-BiO）训练量子神经权重，在六个基准数据集上相比LSTM和EQNN分别降低预测误差36.36%和22.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业云需要精准的工作负载预测和主动资源预留，但传统机器学习（CML）模型学习能力有限，难以应对多样、高维且资源需求突变的工作负载，导致功耗过高和资源管理困难。因此需要一种更强预测能力的模型来解决该问题。

Method: 提出QB-HNN模型：将工作负载信息转换为量子比特（qubits），通过由量子比特神经元组成的深度网络传播，采用哈达玛门（Hadamard-gated）激活函数获取叠加态以实现直观模式学习。同时提出量子黑洞双相位优化算法（QB-BiO）用于训练和优化量子神经权重。

Result: 使用三种异构类型云工作负载的六个基准数据集，与五种最先进方法对比评估。该模型将预测误差较现有LSTM方法降低36.36%，较EQNN方法降低22.83%，在广泛工作负载范围内表现出优异的预测性能。

Conclusion: QB-HNN模型融合了量子力学的计算效率与神经网络的学习能力，能有效预测多样化、高维度且突变性的云工作负载，为实现工业云高效资源管理与节能减排提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Accurate workload prediction and proactive resource reservation are crucial for industry clouds. However, the conventional machine learning (CML) models with limited learning capabilities often fail to predict diverse, high-dimensional workloads with sudden changes in resource demand, leading to excessive power consumption and resource management issues. In this context, this article proposes a novel Hadamard neural network with quantum blackhole (QB-HNN) optimization. This model combines the computational efficiency of quantum mechanics with the persuasive learning capability of neural networks (NNs). The workload information is transformed into qubits and propagated via a deep network of qubit neurons comprising a Hadamard-gated activation function to fetch superposition within the QB-HNN model for intuitive pattern learning. Furthermore, a novel quantum blackhole biphase optimization (QB-BiO) algorithm is introduced to train and optimize qubit neural weights. The performance of the proposed model is comprehensively evaluated and compared with five state-of-the-art approaches using six benchmark datasets of three heterogeneous varieties of cloud workloads. The prediction accuracy achieved for an extensive range of workloads confirms its influential performance by minimizing the prediction error up to 36.36% and 22.83% over existing LSTM- and EQNN-based prediction approaches, respectively.

</details>


### [413] [Scalable Multi-GPU Simulation of 3D Multicellular Growth with RNN-Based Workload Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25890)
*Matvey Moisseyev,Huijing Du,Dandan Zheng,Chi Zhang,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的多GPU三维多细胞生长模拟框架，结合GPU加速、空间分箱、域分解和负载感知分区，并引入基于RNN的负载均衡控制器来应对细胞运动、生长和分裂导致的动态负载失衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于亚细胞元模型（SEM）的多细胞生长模拟能捕捉复杂的组织发育过程，但元素级交互带来的计算成本高昂。此外，细胞运动、生长和分裂会持续重塑空间工作负载分布，导致初始均衡的分区随时间推移变得低效，亟需一种能感知历史信息并动态调整的负载均衡策略。

Method: 提出一个多GPU框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) GPU加速结合空间分箱（spatial binning）加速交互计算；(2) 域分解将模拟空间划分为多个子域；(3) 工作负载感知的分区策略；(4) 基于RNN的负载均衡控制器，该控制器观察近期各rank的执行时间和分区状态，学习对反应式边界调整规则的残差修正。控制器在带有随机化工作负载动态的可微分替代环境中离线训练，无需真实执行轨迹。

Result: GPU加速结合空间分箱将交互计算速度较串行CPU基线提升约三个数量级。RNN引导的负载均衡将平均全局失衡度从静态分区下的11.3%降至3.5%，端到端运行时间较静态分区降低9.0%，相较于反应式基线将切片迁移量减少7.7倍。

Conclusion: 历史感知的负载均衡控制能够在避免不必要重分区的同时有效改善工作负载均衡度，验证了该框架在计算可扩展性和负载均衡效果方面的有效性，并通过胚胎表皮发育用例展示了其针对空间和时间演化工作负载场景的适用性。

Abstract: Detailed multicellular growth simulations based on subcellular element models (SEMs) can capture complex tissue development, but their element-level interactions impose substantial computational cost. This work presents a scalable multi-GPU framework for 3D multicellular growth simulation that combines GPU acceleration, spatial binning, domain decomposition, and workload-aware partitioning. Cell movement, growth, and division continuously reshape the spatial workload distribution, causing initially balanced partitions to become inefficient over time. To address this, we introduce an RNN-based load-balancing controller that observes recent per-rank execution times and partition states and learns residual corrections to a reactive boundary-adjustment rule. The controller is trained offline in a differentiable surrogate of the load-balancing loop with randomized workload dynamics, requiring no measured execution traces for training. We evaluate the framework in terms of single-GPU acceleration, multi-GPU computation scaling, controller-level load-balancing behavior, and end-to-end simulation performance, with comparisons against static partitioning, reactive load balancing, and conventional time-series prediction baselines. A representative embryonic epidermal development use case further demonstrates the type of spatially and temporally evolving workload targeted by the framework. In our evaluation, GPU acceleration with spatial binning accelerates the interaction computation by roughly three orders of magnitude over a serial CPU baseline. RNN-guided load balancing reduces the mean global imbalance from 11.3% under static partitioning to 3.5%, lowers end-to-end runtime by 9.0% relative to static partitioning, and reduces slice migration by 7.7x compared with the reactive baseline, showing that history-aware control can improve workload balance while avoiding unnecessary repartitioning.

</details>


### [414] [Slasher: Power Flexibility for Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26021)
*Liuzixuan Lin,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Jaylen Wang,Hassan Khan,Rodrigo L. Assis,Mariana Rodrigues,Kyle Woolcock,Nithish Mahalingam,Brijesh Warrier,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Slasher，一个用于Azure数据中心电力调制的通用系统，可处理从单个机架到区域多数据中心电网事件的多种功率调节场景，通过协调数据中心资源在满足功率目标的同时最小化对工作负载的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心消耗大量电力，经常面临需要调节功率的场景（如基础设施故障、电网故障、电网服务等），这些场景在功率幅度、削减范围、通知时间等多个维度上需求各异，需要一个通用的系统来应对。

Method: 回顾主要功率调制场景，使用生产云数据中心的数据刻画功率削减杠杆，描述Slasher的系统架构，形式化云数据中心功率调制控制问题，并开发高保真数据中心模拟器和提出工作负载影响模型用于设计和评估功率控制算法。

Result: 提出并构建了Slasher系统，能够协调数据中心资源以在不同规模（机架级到区域多数据中心级）的场景中满足功率目标，同时通过模拟器和影响模型对功率控制算法进行了设计和评估。

Conclusion: Slasher为数据中心功率调制提供了通用解决方案，能够覆盖从单个机架到区域性电网事件的多尺度场景，有效权衡功率目标与工作负载影响。

Abstract: Datacenters consume many megawatts of power, and regularly encounter scenarios that require modulating their power draw. These scenarios include datacenter infrastructure failures, power grid failures, grid services, and more, spanning a diverse range of requirements in terms of the power magnitude, the scope of the reduction, the notice time, and other dimensions. To address these scenarios, we have built Slasher, a general system for modulating the power of \azure datacenters to handle scenarios ranging from individual racks to regional multi-datacenter grid events. Slasher coordinates datacenter resources with the goal of meeting power targets while minimizing negative impact on hosted workloads.
  In this paper, we review the main power modulation scenarios, characterize the power reduction levers using data from production cloud datacenters, describe Slasher's system architecture, and formulate the cloud datacenter power modulation control problem. We also develop a high-fidelity datacenter simulator and propose a workload impact model, using them to design and evaluate power control algorithms.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.BM'></div>

# q-bio.BM [[Back]](#toc)

### [415] [A meta-algorithm for ab initio reconstruction of complex mixtures in cryo-EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25388)
*Alkin Kaz,Arda Kaz,Ellen D. Zhong*

Main category: q-bio.BM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的方法，用于在冷冻电镜（cryo-EM）重建中生成和聚合多类作业，将从业者常用的迭代分类和过滤策略形式化，能够对包含数十种不同物种的异质样本进行从头重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统cryo-EM重建面对异质、不纯样本时依赖专家临时性（ad hoc）的分类和过滤策略，缺乏系统化方法处理包含多种不同物种的复杂样本，无法自动化扩展到大规模多类别重建任务。

Method: 提出一种系统化的方法，用于生成和聚合多类cryo-EM重建作业。该方法将标准临时性的迭代分类和过滤策略形式化，通过并行计算实现多类别的从头重建，并具备随计算资源扩展的能力。

Result: 在Tomotwin-100数据集的45类子集上从头重建准确率达97%，在完整Tomotwin-100数据集上准确率达75%，并成功从未过滤的实验cryo-EM数据集中恢复核糖体组装状态。

Conclusion: 该方法首次成功实现对包含数十种不同物种的数据集进行从头重建，计算能力可随资源扩展，为现代实验环境中的自动化cryo-EM工作流程奠定了基础。

Abstract: We describe a systematic approach for spawning and aggregating multi-class cryo-EM reconstruction jobs. This approach formalizes standard ad hoc strategies of iterative classification and filtering typically used by practitioners to sort impure, heterogeneous samples. To our knowledge, this is the first method that can successfully perform ab initio reconstruction on datasets containing dozens of distinct species. We obtain 97% accuracy on ab initio reconstruction of a 45-class subset of Tomotwin-100, 75% accuracy on the full Tomotwin-100 dataset, and demonstrate recovery of ribosomal assembly states from an unfiltered experimental cryo-EM dataset. Our approach's capability scales with compute and lays the foundation for automated cryo-EM workflows in modern experimental settings.

</details>
